| the CEM | 2,179 ± 131 | −123 ± 157 | −620 ± 192 | 0.30 / 0.45 / 0.25 | 171 | 4.18 | 264 | 1,156 | 18.63 (+1.2 ± 1.5) | 16.88 | 8 of 20 |
| the withholder | 964 ± 49 | −1,338 ± 132 | — | 0 / 0 / 1 | 0 | — | 0 | 479 (−684 ± 73) | 19.37 (+1.9 ± 1.5) | 19.18 (+2.1 ± 0.7) | 0 |

The rounds: the direct +643, +195, +224, +340, +309 against the ratchet, its mix steady at 85–89% undercut and 2–4% withheld from the second round; the CEM −89, −93, −20, −23, −123, its mix 0.30 / 0.45–0.49 / 0.19–0.29, wandering about zero. (The direct's model: `direct/models/direct-online-10.pt`; the world model's `wm/models/wm-online-10.pt`.)

**The gate.** The rule since run 23: the learner must beat the careful outside noise, and sit within noise of the ideal, or its result is not a finding (the chain's gate: more than 2 SE above the ratchet, within 3 SE of the ideal, on the same seeds). **The direct clears, by the width of a hair** — +309 ± 154 is 2.006 SE — and sits 1.3 SE short of the ideal: it learned the undercut (89% of its decisions, 222 awards against the ideal's 228, the same bid over cost, the same wealth held) and the tenth it does not cut is the gap. **The world model fails on both branches.** The CEM plainly: a quarter of its ticks withheld and a third at the ratchet's bid, −123 ± 157, the rounds never above zero. The head alone reads +330 ± 214, 1.5 SE above the ratchet — inside noise by the rule — and −167 ± 167 against the ideal; it wins on 13 seeds of 20 where the ideal wins on 15, and the gap is one action: **it withholds one decision in five** (0 to 52% by seed) where the ideal never does. By the method ruling's branch (the direct clears and the model does not) the model is at fault; by the stopping rule no planner work. The line under the rules runs under the direct (as the ruling after run 29 had it for the parent), and the head alone's line beside it as a reading only — uncalibrated by the gate, so by the ruling after run 23 its no-win would not be a finding, a win would stand.

**The head's fault, read on four seeds on-policy** (seeds 24, 25, 36 — withholding 46%, 45%, 52% — and 31, withholding none; the head's per-action read at every bid decision, 1,512 of them). The withholding is a **late-run trap**: none between ticks 200 and 400, then 38–77% of the decisions in every hundred ticks from 400 on. At the decisions it withholds the head reads the undercut −1.6 and the ratchet −2.9 (against +6.6 and +0.8 where it undercuts); the states are the **thin margin** — the slot's cost above a fifth of the price level (seed 31's slot is cheaper, 0.12–0.20, and the head never withholds there; p1 +11.2), the market price below 2.5 times the slot's cost (65% withheld, the undercut read at zero), the price level below two thirds of P_0 (69–83%) — **and idleness**: once idle for more than 2.6 ticks the head withholds 93 times in 100 and reads the undercut −1.6, so a withhold makes the next one. The training signal in those same states says the opposite: on the offline set the undercut's attributed reward is **positive everywhere the head withholds** — +1.3 ± 0.15 at a price over cost below 2.2 (won 76 times in 100), +2.2 at 2.2–2.5, +3.75 ± 0.5 after 2.6–9 idle ticks (361 decisions against the ratchet's 1,981 and the withholder's thousands in that corner — the undercutter is rarely idle, so the corner is thin for it), +2.6 at a price level below 0.58 P_0 — and the ratchet's smaller but positive too. The head extrapolates the thin corner below zero and the direct, on the same kind of signal, does not (2% withheld). It is the latent's reading, not the signal's, and the stopping rule says not to work on it; the direct carries the line.

**Hoarding and P_t, as ruled.** The undercutter holds +152 ± 85 real more a tick than the ratchet — its earnings, 4.5 periods' worth of them, not a stock withheld from the market: its unlocked balance is the ratchet's plus the gain — and the direct the same (+164 ± 68). The price level at the close does not move with the one slot's bidding (+0.2 ± 1.7 for the ideal, +0.5 ± 1.6 for the direct; its awards are 9% of the run's), nor the members' mean price (+0.6 ± 0.7). The withholder's absence does move them: P_t +1.9 ± 1.5, the members' price +2.1 ± 0.7 (the cheapest slot's work goes to costlier ratchets), and its own wealth held falls to 479. Under the rules (§39) the undercutter's P_t read −0.3; the question of a bidder moving the price level is answered by the hand already: one slot cannot.

**The cell's limit, for the record.** The hand found one cell at 3.0 SE on a ridge, and its noise is the cell's own: the ideal's gain by seed runs from −1,045 to +1,995 against the ratchet (SE 168 real on twenty seeds), so a learner at two thirds of the ideal's gain reads 2 SE on this budget and one at a third reads nothing. The gate separated the three learners the way their mixes do — the direct at the undercut, the head alone a fifth withheld, the CEM a quarter withheld and a third at the ratchet — and that is what it is for. The suite at `14ba882e` (the driver's bidder role) passes in all three modes: 342 passed, 0 failed, 32 expected failures (`results/tests/14ba882e.txt`, `14ba882e-record.txt`).

**Launched:** the line under the rules (`results/redteam/Q6/line-v4/`: run 24's world, λ 0.2, `--undercut 0.1`, the cheapest slot; the collection on seeds 1–20 shared, each learner on its own copy): the direct (`direct/`, ten rounds, the final against the ratchet, the undercut and the withholder) and the world model under the head alone (`wm/`, `online --plan greedy`, the final with the CEM beside). Read in §43. The reference under the rules: §39's 10% undercut at λ 0.2 was not run by hand (the hand ran δ 0.2 there, +174 ± 130); the final's `undercut` row is that reference.

## 42. Q7 built — the learned requester — and its references by hand on the dispute baseline (October 25, 2026; `results/redteam/Q7/refs/`)

**The construction** (`--learner-role requester`; the driver and the package only, nothing in the engine; the brief's Q7: "one learned requester maximizing tasks per credit; expected: it rediscovers the floor and stops"; per the ruling after run 30, on the dispute baseline — run 24's world with truthful disputes, §38). The slot is the first standing requester (`--requester-slot`), a requester and not an actor: it has no standing, reviews nothing, and has no boundary decision (a requester's departure buys it nothing). It is asked at each posting the population's rule admits (fewer than K_open of its requests open) **which rule its ceiling follows**: 0 the **market rule's**, the population's (1.2 B̄ — the reference, `careful`); 1 **patient** — 0.7 B̄, raised to the state's price max(B̄, the floor) after `--patient-ticks` (20) unserved, run 17's customer; 2 **the floor** — the state's reservation (8, nominal), never raised: 2.4.0 §8.7's posting, which only a member bidding under it serves (the state's price is above it, so the state never does, and its clock does not run). Its disputes follow the population's rule (truthful). The case a decision posts is credited to that decision, so the attributed training reward carries the case's events to the posting that made it — the price paid, the two pots, the base fee, the refunds at closure, a guarantee received when a delivery fails, a court fee — each labelled.

**The measure, declared.** Tasks per credit is a ratio; the learner needs an additive one. The requester's lifetime is its **surplus v·F − S**: F the tasks received — its settled deliveries whose truth is fulfilled, whatever the verdict (the requester holds the result; a fulfilled delivery rejected is a task received for nothing, a bad one approved is paid for nothing); S the credits spent, summed from its own events at the index of each (`req_read_events`: the wallet's revaluation under the moving index is not a spend — the first cut read the wallet's change and the revaluation swamped it — nor is the issuance of 10 a tick); v the value of a task received, `--task-value`, in real credits. **Read at v = the careful requester's own credits per task**, the measure is tasks per credit linearized at the careful's rate: the careful reads about zero by construction, a requester that buys tasks cheaper reads above it, one that buys fewer tasks at the same price reads zero. The ratio itself is read beside (`req_tasks_per_1000`), with the price paid, the wait, the postings the wallet refused, the deliveries paid for nothing and the state's deliveries. The observation (`obs.build_obs_req`, 45 fields): the market and the dial (the first 16 fields of every role's vector), then its wallet, its requests open and waiting and the oldest's age, the three ceilings with their all-in and affordability, its record as a requester (posted, received, paid for nothing, final N, the state's deliveries, spent, tasks per credit, the wait, the price paid, disputes, raises, its last rule) and the period's remainder.

**The references on the dispute baseline** (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6 --dispute-policy truthful`, seeds 21–40, paired against the market rule; v read from the careful's own run: S/F = 30.9 real a task):

| policy | postings asked / posted / refused by the wallet | tasks received | spent (real) | real a task | tasks per 1,000 | price paid | wait to award | raised | paid for nothing | final N | state deliveries | wallet at the close (real) | surplus − careful at v = 31 | P_t at the close (paired) | members' price (paired) | awards (all, paired) | state delivered (paired) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **careful** (the market rule) | 591 / 416 / 175 | 327 | 10,098 ± 91 | 30.9 | 32.4 | 19.36 | 0.06 | 0.45 | 0.5 | 88 | 0.3 | 178 | 0 | 20.9 | 20.0 | 2,445 | 18.4 |
| **patient** (0.7 B̄ → p_T after 20) | 291 / 291 / 0 | 228 | 5,562 ± 193 | 24.5 ± 0.2 | 40.9 | 11.61 | 6.6 | 56 | 0.35 | 60 | 0 | 5,598 | **+1,486 ± 108** (13.8 SE; 20 of 20) | −2.3 ± 0.7 | −1.9 ± 0.2 | −40 ± 28 | −8.3 ± 6.0 |
| **floor** (the reservation, never raised) | 188 / 188 / 0 | 146 | 3,049 ± 285 | 20.7 ± 0.3 | 48.6 | 7.35 | 16.3 | 0 | 0.35 | 39 | 0 | 9,145 | **+1,428 ± 142** (10.1 SE; 19 of 20) | −4.1 ± 0.7 | −3.0 ± 0.3 | −120 ± 31 | −8.1 ± 6.2 |

**What the hand says.** Under the rules the market rule's requester is plainly beatable on tasks per credit, and the brief's expectation reads true: the price a requester pays is set by its ceiling. The careful requester pays 19.4 a delivery and 30.9 real a task all told (the pots' unrefunded part, the fee, the deliveries that fail), spends its whole wallet (175 of its 591 admitted postings refused by the wallet, 178 real left at the close) and gets 327 tasks. The patient requester's ceiling at 0.7 B̄ admits only the cheaper members' bids and pays 11.6 a delivery, 24.5 a task; a fifth of its postings wait the 20 ticks and are raised to the state's price. The floor's posting at the reservation (8 nominal, 7.35 paid — the cheapest members' ratchets bid under it) pays 20.7 a task, a third less than the careful, and is served by a member every time (the state never, since the state's price is B̄): it buys tasks cheapest — 48.6 per 1,000 real against 32.4 — and buys the fewest, 146, because it waits 16 ticks for each award and the population's K_open rule lets it hold five at a time; it ends the run with 9,145 real unspent. **At the careful's own rate the two cheaper rules read alike** — +1,486 ± 108 and +1,428 ± 142 against the careful, −58 ± 114 between them — and the measure's v decides between them (at 25 the floor leads by 440, at 40 the patient leads by 800 and the floor reads −205 ± 253): where a task is worth more than the careful pays for one, waiting sixteen ticks for the cheapest member costs more tasks than it saves credits. The learner's action set lets it do what neither does — post at the floor while the queue is short and at the patient's ceiling when it is not, by the market it sees — so a win in the line above both would be the learner's. **What it costs the loop, by the hand:** one requester in six at the floor pulls the price level down 4.1 ± 0.7 at the close (a fifth) and the members' mean price 3.0 ± 0.3; at the patient's ceiling 2.3 and 1.9 — run 17's mechanism (the ceiling as the price lever, every customer patient collapsing P_t to 4.8) read at a sixth of the demand, and the state's reservation at 8 the place where it would stop; the state's deliveries fall by 8 of 18 (the cheaper requester's work goes to members), the awards by 40 and 120 (the fewer postings), the false approvals do not move, the members idle for a bond do not move.

**Launched** (`results/redteam/Q7/line-v4/`): the question's cell is its calibration cell, since the ideal clears there by the hand's reading — the collection on seeds 1–20 (careful / patient / floor / ε / random), the world model under the CEM with the head alone read in the final and the direct side by side on its own copy, ten rounds, the final on seeds 21–40 against the careful, the patient and the floor at v = 31 (the gate's ideal the patient, the floor within noise of it). Read in §44, after the Q6 line's §43. The suite at `5a079b80` (the driver's requester role) passes in all three modes.

The rounds, against the ratchet: the direct +298, +401, +308, +426, +290 (its mix 0.16–0.19 / 0.71–0.78 / 0.06–0.09 from the first round read); the head alone +202, −19, +246, +265, +231 (0.09–0.15 / 0.71–0.79 / 0.12–0.15). The direct's model `direct/models/direct-online-10.pt`, the world model's `wm/models/wm-online-10.pt`. No slot departed (the head alone 2 leaves in 20 runs, the CEM 3 — the broke slots' negative stay reads, as §40).

**The reference under the rules, first.** §41 recorded that §39's grid had no 10% cut at the rule's λ (the hand ran δ 0.2 there, +174 ± 130, and δ 0.1 only at λ 1.0, 2.0, 4.0); the line's final is that reference, and **it clears the rule's three SE: +275 ± 88 (3.1 SE), winning on 14 seeds of 20.** §39's headline — "under the rules the ratchet is not plainly undercuttable" — was the hand's grid speaking, and the grid had a hole at the cell that matters; the hand's finding under the rules is corrected here: **on the cheapest slot a 10% cut under the market price beats the ratchet plainly.** Why 10% and not 20%: at the rule's λ selection weighs price lightly, so the shallower cut wins the same awards as the deeper one (193 against §39's 194) while giving up half as much price — the ratchet bids 4.83 times the slot's cost, the 10% cut 4.65, and the extra 55 awards a run (+40%) carry +653 real of delivery income against +242 of effort burned and +97 of guarantees forfeited on the extra deliveries (ρ 0.866 against 0.894); the slot's reviewing does not fall (188 reviews against 186), so §39's "the reviewing forgone while committed" does not bite at this depth. The ratchet's margin rule is the drivers' population rule, not a rule of the economy: the finding is of the §19 kind "a strategy performs badly" — an evaluation under the fixed rules — and what it says of the economy is below.

**The learners.** The brief asked whether a learned bidder undercuts the ideal where it plainly pays. **The direct does, and the win stands by the rule**: +290 ± 101 against the ratchet (2.9 SE; the gate's 2 SE cleared, where its calibration cleared by a hair), +15 ± 88 against the hand's undercut — it is the undercut, found: the cut 76% of its ticks, the ratchet's bid 17%, withheld 7%, 189 awards against the hand's 193, the same income, the same wealth held, wins on 15 seeds of 20 where the hand wins on 14. Its 17% at the ratchet's bid costs it nothing measurable against the hand — those are ticks where the ratchet's bid and the cut are close or the market thin. **The head alone wins too**, +231 ± 112 (2.1 SE; −44 ± 132 against the undercut), on 15 seeds of 20 — uncalibrated by §41's gate, so by the ruling after run 23 its win stands while its no-win would not have been one; it withholds 13% here against §41's 19% (the rule's λ keeps the market price nearer the slot's cost less often — the late-run trap's corner is thinner), and the withheld eighth is the gap to the direct. The CEM beside loses as in the calibration (−133 ± 132, a third withheld, 9 wins): the planner's residual, not worked on by the stopping rule.

**Hoarding and P_t, as ruled, under the rules.** The undercutters hold +180–190 real more a tick than the ratchet — the direct +189 ± 59, the hand +182 ± 52 — and it is their earnings, as in §41 (the lifetime gain is 275–290; the balance is the ratchet's plus the gain accruing), not a stock withheld: nothing is kept from the market, their awards are a tenth more than the ratchet's and the offers lost fewer. **The price level does not move** with the one slot's cut: +0.12 ± 0.65 for the direct, +0.15 ± 0.58 for the hand (the head alone −0.80 ± 0.54, 1.5 SE, its withheld ticks' doing if anything), the members' mean price −0.3 to −0.4 ± 0.3–0.4 — the slot's awards are 8% of the run's at a 4% cut. The run's awards read +58 ± 31 under the direct and +42 ± 36 under the hand (1.2–1.9 SE: the cheapest slot taking roots that otherwise waited, within noise). The economy's reading: one member undercutting the population's ratchet earns a seventh more on its own account and moves nothing public — the ratchet's rule is beatable, the price level is not, from one slot. Whether a population of undercutters moves P_t is run 13's question (supply → cost), not this one's.

**Q6's answer.** The ratchet the reference, undercutting the ideal where it plainly pays, hoarding and P_t beside — as ruled: it plainly pays on the cheapest slot under the rules at a 10% cut (the hand, 3.1 SE), the learned bidder finds it (the direct, 2.9 SE, within noise of the hand; the head alone 2.1 SE), there is no hoarding (the wealth held is the gain), and the price level does not move. **A win, the first in the learned lines**, and it is the hand's finding too — the learner rediscovered a better bid than the population's rule, which is what the brief's Q6 asked; nothing in it is a rule of the economy. Q6 closes on it unless the reviewer wants the population cell (every careful member at the undercut's rule, a hand-written sweep — not launched).

The rounds, against the careful: the CEM +1,418, +1,439, +1,353, +1,260, +1,443, its mix 0.03–0.11 / 0.31–0.50 / 0.41–0.59 (the floor's share rising through round 8, the patient's back in the final); the direct +1,278, +1,402, +1,161, +1,303, +1,280, its mix 0.01–0.02 / 0.12–0.21 / 0.78–0.87 from the first round. The false approvals (+0.45 ± 0.59, −0.15 ± 0.49, −0.10 ± 0.42) and the members idle for a bond (+17 ± 41, −24 ± 37, −34 ± 33) do not move under any of them. (`wm/models/wm-online-10.pt`, `direct/models/direct-online-10.pt`; `q7-gate.txt` beside the finals.)

**The gate: all three clear**, each more than 2 SE above the careful and within 3 SE of the ideal — the CEM within a third of an SE of the patient (−42 ± 132) and of the floor (+16 ± 119), the head alone 1.3 SE short, the direct 1.5 SE short. At v = 31 the patient and the floor are the same surplus within noise (§42), so the ideal is a line, not a point, and every learner sits on it: **none beats the hand, none is distinguishable from it.** The brief's expectation — *it rediscovers the floor and stops* — reads true to the letter: **the market rule is abandoned** (6% of the CEM's postings, 0.1% of the head alone's, 1% of the direct's — the careful's rule is the worst of the three on every seed and the learners find that in the first round) and the learner posts at the reservation or at the patient ceiling and nowhere else. The three differ in how much floor: the direct 87%, the head alone 81%, the CEM half, with the other half patient — by seed the CEM runs from 14% floor (seed 31) to 94% (seed 25), and that is the ideal's indifference showing through: where v makes patient and floor alike, a learner has no gradient between them and the mix is the seed's noise. On the ratio read beside — tasks per 1,000 real, the brief's own words — the direct is the best requester in the table (49.7 against the floor's 48.6 and the careful's 32.4): the more floor, the more tasks a credit, and the surplus at v = 31 does not reward it because at the careful's rate a task is worth what the patient pays for it plus its wait.

**Where it stops.** The floor action is the construction's lowest — the reservation itself, 8 nominal, never raised (§42: a posting below it the state never serves and its clock does not run) — so "stops at the floor" is read here as: offered the market rule, the patient ceiling and the reservation, the learner never posts above the patient's and sits at the reservation as much as the ideal lets it, and nothing in the three learners' behaviour reaches for a ceiling the construction does not offer. Whether a requester would go *under* the reservation (served only by a member bidding under it, the state out) is a hand-written cell (not launched); the brief's question was rediscovery, and the floor it rediscovers is 2.4.0's.

**What it costs the loop** (paired against the careful's world). One requester in six at the floor half the time and the patient ceiling the rest: the price level at the close −3.6 ± 0.7 (a sixth: 20.9 → 17.3; the floor's hand −4.1, the patient's −2.3, the learner between), the members' mean price −2.5 to −2.8 ± 0.4 (an eighth), the run's awards −107 to −174 (4–7%: the learner buys 126–156 tasks where the careful bought 327, at 2,600–3,400 real instead of 10,098, and leaves 8,400–9,000 real unspent), the state's deliveries within noise (−5 ± 7.5, −9.5 ± 6.3, +4.8 ± 8.1, from 18 a run), the false approvals and the members idle for a bond unchanged. Run 17's mechanism — a patient customer at 0.7 B̄ driving the price to 4.8 and the state to 86% of the work — does not recur: with the reservation in force the learned requester's pressure on the price stops where 2.4.0 put the stop, the floor holds at 8 nominal with P_t at 17.3, the state's share of the work does not rise (the floor is never served by the state, by rule), and the cheapest members' ratchets serve the floor at a 15-tick wait. That is the reservation doing what it was for, read by a learner rather than by the hand.

**Q7's answer, as the brief asked it.** One learned requester maximizing tasks per credit rediscovers the floor and stops: all three learners clear the gate (the CEM +1,443 ± 147 against the market rule's requester, on the ideal's line), abandon the market rule in the first round, and post at the reservation and the patient ceiling; on tasks per credit the direct, the most at the floor, buys 50 a thousand real against the market rule's 32. The measure is the implementer's construction (the surplus at the careful's own rate; §42) and is so marked for the reviewer. The cost to the loop is the floor's cost, bounded by the reservation; nothing unexpected in it. Run once, as the brief said. Q6 (§43) and Q7 close on their findings; the brief's questions are read through.

## 45. The stability track's first cells (October 25, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/`) — the undercutting population, every careful member and half; a requester posting under the reservation

**The ruling after run 31** (recorded): Q6 and Q7 closed on their findings; Q7's measure confirmed as constructed (v the declared knob, the ratio beside). Two cells launched as evaluations under fixed rules, the stability track's (the brief's §5) first: (1) the population at the 10% cut — every careful member, and half — against the ratchet's: P_t, the members' real wealth, the customers' hoards, the state's share, the busy line, honesty, and whether the reservation binds from below; (2) a requester posting under the reservation — the share of demand clearing below the state's floor and its cost to the members serving it. Then learned populations proper.

**The construction** (`b8c9186a`; the driver only, nothing in the engine or the package): `--bid undercut` — the careful members in `--undercut-share` (every one, or half drawn once on their own stream at genesis and at each careful arrival; the stampers and the arrivals' other kinds keep the ratchet) bid Q6's rule, (1 − δ) B̄ floored at their own cost, δ 0.1; `--ceiling-policy under-floor --ceiling-requesters 1` — the first standing requester (Q7's slot, one in six) posts at `--under-floor-frac` of the state's reservation, never raised; by 2.4.0 the state never serves it and its clock does not run while it waits there, so it clears only on a member's bid under the floor. The readings added to the row: the undercutters' awards and those at their cost (the cut floored), their real wealth against the ratchets' beside them; the awards at and under the floor, the member's margin on them (the bid over its cost), the members serving under the floor and their real wealth, the final N and guarantees lost on those deliveries; the under-floor requests posted, awarded, waiting, their wait and price, fulfilled, final N; the state's share of the deliveries, the requesters' wallets real, the state's price over the market (positive when B̄ is under the floor — the reservation binding from below). Cell (1) on run 24's world (Q6's), cell (2) on the dispute baseline (Q7's); seeds 21–40, paired by seed against the ratchet and the wallet rule (`drivers.sweep`, one factor each). The control reproduces the committed dispute baseline's seed 1 to the decimal; the suite at `b8c9186a` passes in three modes (`results/tests/b8c9186a.txt`, `-record.txt`).

The price level's path, the mean over seeds at every hundred ticks — the ratchet: 20.0, 19.3, 20.6, 21.6, 17.5, 18.1, 19.0, 19.8, 20.4, 20.6; **every member undercutting: 20.0, 15.7, 17.2, 15.9, 10.1, 9.1, 8.8, 8.9, 8.7, 8.7**; half: 20.0, 17.1, 21.6, 19.4, 16.1, 16.6, 17.9, 18.5, 19.5, 20.0. The close by seed under every member undercutting: 6.7, 6.8, 6.8, 7.2, 7.4, 7.8, 8.0, 8.0, 8.7, 8.8, 8.9, 8.9, 8.9, 9.1, 9.2, 9.9, 10.0, 10.1, 10.8, 11.4.

**Does it settle, oscillate or collapse; where; is the floor met** (the brief's §5 question, by hand). **Every careful member at the 10% cut collapses the price to the cost of work and settles there**: the cut compounds through the periods — each period's B̄ is 90% of the last where the members' costs allow — until the bids hit the costs; by tick 600 P_t is 8.7 ± 0.3 and stays, no oscillation (the last four readings 8.8, 8.9, 8.7, 8.7), the members' mean price 11.7 nominal. Where: **at the anchor cost** — 8, the median of the drawn costs, which is also the reservation's value — a third of the undercutters' awards go at their own cost (the cut floored), and the price settles where the cheapest half of the members bid cost. **The reservation binds from below on 6 seeds of 20**: the close's B̄ sits under 8 (6.7–7.8) and the state's price is the floor, 0.27 over the market on average; on the other 14 it grazes the floor from above (8.0–11.4). The state's share does not rise (0.5% against 0.9%): the floor stops the *state's* price, not the members' — a request at β ≥ 8 is served by a member bidding under 8 before the state is summoned, and a quarter of the run's awards clear under the floor (24% against 4%). **Half the careful members at the cut do not move the price**: −0.5 ± 0.8 at the close (the path dips to 16 at tick 500 and recovers to 20.0), the members' mean price −1.4 ± 0.3 — the ratchets set the level and the undercutters bid a tenth under it, taking 47% of the awards (their share of the members) and almost never at cost (4.5%). The economy's stability reading: the price is stable under a minority of undercutters and collapses to cost under a majority; the collapse is monotone and settles, and the floor is where it stops — from above on most seeds, from below on a third.

**Who gains.** Under every member undercutting the members' nominal wealth halves (615 against 1,329) and **their delivery income falls under their effort cost** (918 against 1,437 nominal a life — effort costs are nominal, the anchor, and the price fell through them): the members work at a loss and live on their reviewing fees and reimbursements. The real measure, which deflates by the price of work, reads them *richer* (+137 ± 54) — the deflator is the collapsed price itself, so a credit buys twice the work; the reading is right as a measure of work bought and wrong as a measure of the members' position, and the nominal line is the one to read here. **The gain is the customers'**: a requester's wallet at the close is 3,119 nominal unlocked against 99 — the customers pay 60% less a task, buy 7% more tasks, and hoard the rest (the issuance of 10 a tick accumulating unspent): 7,505 real a requester against 112, on every seed. Ω's fee income falls by 37% with the price (the fee is a share of the price) and its net position by 1,487. Entry slows (arrivals 19.0 against 19.9: a candidate reads the margin B̄ − cost against its reservation and finds less) and departures rise by two. Honesty does not move: the false approvals −0.3 ± 1.2, the false rejections +0.8 ± 1.8 (the overturned reviews rise with the volume, +2.1 on +43 reviews). The busy line is empty — nothing marked, nothing declined, nothing refused — but the ticks with no free member rise from 9 to 58: the market is busier at the lower price, and the members idle for want of a bond rise by 99 ticks (smaller wallets at a smaller bond). Under half: the undercutters end where the control's careful do (1,279 against 1,291) and **the ratchets beside them gain** (+213 ± 55 — unpaired by actor, the costs drawn; the ratchets keep their price on the awards reputation gives them and the undercutters carry the volume); §43's one undercutter among ratchets gained +275, half a population of them gain nothing each — undercutting pays alone and not in company, the classic reading.

**The share of demand clearing below the floor.** Under the wallet rule alone 3.3% of the run's awards already clear under the reservation: the cheapest members' ratchets start at cost × 1.3 — 5 to 7 nominal for a cost of 4 to 5 — and the award is at the bid, so a cheap member's bid under 8 wins at that bid whatever the ceiling (the floor is the *state's* reservation, not a price floor on the members — the economy's rule, read). The requester under the floor at 6.0 posts 89 requests a run (K_open 5 holds four or five waiting at every tick; the clock does not run on them), is served 84 times at 5.68 after a **54-tick wait** by the members whose ratchet is under 6, and its arrival takes the run's share of demand clearing under the floor to 9.7%: its own 84 awards and 55 more from the other requesters, because the price level it pulls down (−3.9 ± 0.9, §42's floor requester's −4.1 — the demand withdrawn, not the price posted: the requester buys 84 tasks where the wallet rule bought 400, and the idle ratchets decay) brings more of the ratchets under 8. At 4.0 the requester is all but shut out — 18 postings, 13 served at 3.82 after **112 ticks**, five always waiting — and the demand withdrawn still pulls P_t −2.8 ± 1.0 and the state's deliveries to 4 from 36. The under-floor requester's tasks per credit: 66 fulfilled of 84 at 5.68 nominal ≈ 14 a hundred real against the market rule's 3.2 (§42) — the cheapest tasks in any cell, bought slowest.

**Its cost to the members serving it.** The members who serve under the floor earn **1.30 nominal over their cost a delivery** (the control's under-floor servers 1.33 — the same margin; at 4.0 the ceiling, 1.14), where the members' mean price is 17 against a mean cost of 8 — a seventh of an ordinary award's margin — and they carry the guarantee: 21% of those deliveries end in final N (the population's rate: 45.5 of 218) and forfeit 163 nominal of guarantees a run, 0.75 a delivery against the 1.30 margin. Net, a delivery under the floor brings its member about half a credit; the 17.5 members who serve there (2.5 more than in the control) end 126 ± 52 real poorer than the control's under-floor servers (unpaired by actor), the careful members as a whole −47 ± 29. The cost is the thin margin and the bond at risk on it, and the opportunity — a member committed at 5.68 for τ ticks is not free for an award at 17 — which is why only the ratchets already under 8 serve it: nobody bids under cost, and the members above 6 never see the request. The state's share falls (0.8% from 1.5%, 0.2% at 4.0), the false approvals do not move, the members idle for a bond fall with the smaller bonds; the busy line carries the requester's own marks — 108 marked below the reservation, 854 ticks of waiting below it where the clock does not run, no rebate — and nothing else.

**For the record.** Both cells are evaluations under fixed rules; neither is a red-team win (the brief's §5: nothing in it is one) and nothing in them asks for a rule. Two things the reservation's text might be read against: (i) the floor stops the state's price and not the members', so a population undercutting to cost settles at the floor's value because the anchor cost *is* the floor's value — on a world whose costs sat lower the price would settle lower and the state's price would bind from below on every seed; (ii) a requester under the floor is served, slowly and cheaply, by the members whose cost allows it, and its demand withdrawn moves the price more than its price does. The learned populations proper (the brief's §5: honest workers and customers learning to bid, the price the reading) are the next cells; not built — the learner is one slot by construction, and a population of learners is a construction to declare first.

## 46. The learned population, built (October 25, 2026; `c5de6973`) — N learner slots sharing one policy in self-play

**The ruling after run 32** (recorded): both cells readings, no rule. The measure for population cells: the members in nominal surplus, the customers in tasks per credit; the real measure for single-slot lines. The learned population: N learner slots sharing one policy in self-play (each on its own observation; the rounds pool the slots' experience), Q6's policy class, no new calibration — the equilibrium is the reading; the references all-ratchet / all-undercut / half; the readings P_t, the members' price, the nominal surplus, tasks per credit, the state's share, honesty, arrivals and departures, the floor's binding, the action mix by period. The order: (i) bidders; (ii) reviewers in self-play; (iii) bidders and requesters together. The copy-of-one shape is not run (§45's all-undercut cell is it by hand).

**The construction** (the driver and the package only). `--learner-pop all|half` with `--learner-role bidder`: every careful member at genesis is a learner slot — or half, drawn once on the undercutters' stream, so the half is the same half `--undercut-share 0.5` draws on the seed — and a careful arrival joins by the same draw. Each slot is asked its own bid decision once a tick when it is free and a root is for sale its cost fits under, on its own observation (Q6's 45 fields; its own record — awards won, offers lost, the last tick it won — now per slot), and answers with Q6's three actions: the ratchet's bid, the undercut ((1 − δ) B̄ floored at its cost, δ 0.1), withhold. One policy answers every slot's decision (self-play); the collectors store the slot of each decision, the world model's windows run along one slot's own sequence (an episode is N interleaved streams, split by slot), the direct policy trains on every decision as before; the rounds pool all the slots' decisions on the training seeds (~9,500 a run at 28 slots, 20 s a run). The root a slot's bid wins is credited to that slot's decision at that tick (the attribution by case, as Q6); a slot's unclaimed events go to its own lineage. **The exit is the population's rule in population cells** (`--learner-exit rule`): a careful population under the single slot's exit-own rule never departs — the hand-written stay at every boundary — and the all-ratchet reference drifts from the hand's control (P_t 12.7 on seed 21 against 20.2, 28 members staying); under the rule the all-ratchet population reproduces the hand's control to the decimal (seed 21: P_t 20.1982, the members' price 20.2249, 2,409 awards, 8 false approvals, 21 departures), so there is no boundary decision in a population cell and the training signal is the bid decisions' own cases. The all-undercut population reads P_t 9.06 on seed 21 against the hand's 9.99: the learner's rule reads the market price live within the award pass where §45's population read it once a tick — the same equilibrium, recorded.

**The measures, as ruled.** The members' nominal surplus: each slot's wealth now (or its wallet out at departure) less its wealth in (the genesis wealth at the first tick; an arrival's float), summed over the slots, nominal credits (`pop_surplus_nominal`), paired against the all-ratchet on the same seed and slots. The customers' tasks per credit: every requester's settled deliveries whose truth is fulfilled per 1,000 nominal credits spent — the requesters' credits out less in by their own events, the issuance and the locks excluded (`pop_tasks_per_1000`; the market rule's requesters read 32.3 in run 24's world, §42's careful requester's 32.4 real). Beside them in the population's table: P_t and the members' price at the close, the state's share of the deliveries, the awards under the floor and the state's price over the market (the floor's binding), the false approvals, the arrivals and departures, the requesters' wallets, the members idle for a bond, and the action mix by period (ratchet / undercut / withhold over the slots' decisions in each period, pooled over the seeds). The lifetime real summed over the slots stays as the chain's gate reading; it is not the population's measure.

**Launched** (`results/redteam/stability/learned-pop-all/`, `learned-pop-half/`; run 24's world, δ 0.1, seeds 1–20 the collection — careful, undercut, withhold, ε, random — and seeds 21–40 the final): the direct (Q6's line's carrier) in ten rounds with the final against careful (all-ratchet), undercut (all-undercut; in the half cell the half) and the withholder; the world model under the head alone beside, on its own copy of the collection (stride 8). No calibration, as ruled: the equilibrium is the reading. The suite at `c5de6973` in three modes. Read in §47.


## 47. The learned population, read (October 25, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/learned-pop-all/`, `learned-pop-half/`) — the bidders in self-play settle two thirds on the undercut, whatever the company; the price stops at 12, the members lose a third, the customers gain a quarter

Run 24's world, δ 0.1, seeds 21–40 paired against the all-ratchet on the same seeds and slots; the direct policy (Q6's carrier) and the world model's head alone, each in ten online rounds on seeds 1–20 (the final under the round-10 policy); the references by hand on the same slots: the all-ratchet (`careful`, the control to the decimal), the all-undercut (`undercut`, the learner's construction reading the market live within the award pass — §46), the withholder. No calibration, as ruled; the measures the population's (§46): the members' nominal surplus, the customers' tasks per 1,000 nominal credits.

**The equilibrium is one mix, and it does not depend on the company.** Both learners, in both cells, settle on the same rule: the undercut two decisions in three, the ratchet one in four, a tenth withheld — every member learning: the direct 0.25 / 0.66 / 0.09 (ratchet / undercut / withhold), the head alone 0.24 / 0.64 / 0.13; half of them: 0.23 / 0.66 / 0.11 and 0.18 / 0.70 / 0.12. The mix is reached by round 5 and held to round 10 (every member: the paired nominal surplus −8,346 ± 627 at round 5, −8,648 ± 604 at round 10; the head alone −9,019 ± 583 and −8,667 ± 513; the half: −1,054 ± 456 → −807 ± 495 and −1,077 ± 548 → −1,218 ± 459), and within a run it is steady from period 3 on (every member, the direct: 0.44 / 0.46 / 0.10 in the first period, where B̄ is not yet the market's; 0.18 / 0.78 in period 1; then 0.20–0.27 ratchet and 0.57–0.73 undercut through period 13). The same rule at P_t 19 and at P_t 12 says what the policy learned: each slot's decision reads its own award's gain — the undercut wins the root at a smaller margin, the ratchet wins it less often, a withhold is worth zero — and the price the population gives up is spread over every member, the slots included, so no decision sees it. The withhold is chosen where both bids read below zero (the predicted ratchet 0.96 and undercut 2.7 on average; a thin margin with the bond at risk).

**Every careful member learning (`learned-pop-all/`; 27.9 slots a run, 17.4 alive at the close).** The price level falls to 12 and stops there: P_t at the close 12.1 ± 0.6 under the direct and 11.3 ± 0.4 under the head alone (−8.4 ± 0.8 and −9.3 ± 0.6 from 20.6; the seeds 8.9–16.9 and 8.6–14.4), the members' price 14.6 (−5.3 ± 0.3 both) — against the hand's all-undercut on the same slots at 9.07 ± 0.2 (§45's 8.7, the anchor cost) and 11.8. The learned population stops short of the collapse: a quarter of the decisions keep the ratchet and the ratchets set the level, as §45's half showed. The members, nominal, paired: −8,648 ± 604 and −8,667 ± 513 on 26,339 — a third, on every seed of 20; a slot alive ends 968 and 933 nominal against 1,329 (−362 ± 54, −396 ± 47); the all-undercut −15,867 ± 657, three fifths. The slots win more awards (+244 ± 33 and +259 ± 25 on 1,976, an eighth) at a margin 1.78 over cost against 2.49 (−0.72 ± 0.06) and lose nominal on the trade. The real measure reads them richer (+4,620 ± 1,210 and +6,412 ± 1,167), §45's deflator again — the nominal line is the one to read. The customers: tasks per 1,000 nominal credits +9.0 ± 0.5 on 32.2 (28%; the all-undercut +16.8 ± 0.9, 52%), the run's awards +154 ± 32 and +191 ± 26 on 2,449 (6–8%), and the rest hoarded — a requester's wallet 1,781 and 1,735 real against 109 (the all-undercut 3,078). The floor does not bind under the learned population: the state's price over the market 0.00 on every direct seed and on 19 of the head alone's (one seed closes at B̄ 7.04, the floor 0.96 over it; the all-undercut 4 seeds of 20, §45's 6), the awards under the floor 3.5% and 4.2% against the control's 4.0% (the all-undercut 20.5%); the state's share 0.5% and 0.1% against 0.9%. Honesty unmoved: the false approvals +1.7 ± 1.1 and +0.6 ± 1.2 on 11.4 with 6–8% more awards reviewed. The arrivals unchanged (19.9 and 19.8 against 19.9); the departures +2.0 ± 0.3 under both (22.5 against 20.5 — the slots' own, 10.5 against 8.5: the exit rule leaves on income, and two more leave at the lower price); 17.4 slots alive at the close against 19.4; the members idle for a bond 138 and 158 against 179.

**The half (`learned-pop-half/`; half the careful members at genesis and half the careful arrivals, drawn on the undercutters' stream — 13.7 slots a run, 9.4 alive at the close; the references on the same slots: the all-ratchet, the all-undercut, the withholder).** Both learners reach the same equilibrium, and reach it by round 5 (the direct's paired nominal surplus −1,054 ± 456 at round 5 and −807 ± 495 at round 10; the head alone −1,077 ± 548 and −1,218 ± 459): **the undercut two decisions in three** — the direct 0.23 / 0.66 / 0.11 ratchet / undercut / withhold over the run, the head alone 0.18 / 0.70 / 0.12 — with the ratchet kept for a fifth of the decisions and a tenth withheld. By period: the first period half ratchet (0.47–0.54; B̄ not yet the market's), the undercut 80–90% in periods 1–3, a swing back to half in period 4 (0.33 / 0.49 / 0.18) and from there a drift up to 75–80% undercut by periods 9–13, the withhold near a tenth throughout — chosen where both bids read below zero (the attributed signal at a withhold is zero by construction; the predicted ratchet 0.96 and undercut 2.7 on average, the policy withholding where a thin margin and the bond at risk put both under it).

The members, nominal, paired against the all-ratchet on the same seeds and slots: the direct −807 ± 495 (12 seeds of 20 negative), the head alone −1,218 ± 459 (16 of 20), the hand's all-undercut on the same half −2,523 ± 655; a slot alive ends 70 ± 58 and 69 ± 44 nominal poorer (1,242 and 1,244 against 1,313). The learned population wins more awards (+208 ± 29 and +240 ± 32 on 967, a fifth to a quarter more) at a smaller margin (the bid over cost 2.17 and 2.15 against 2.53) and burns more effort for it; the sum is a loss against holding the ratchet together, and a smaller loss than undercutting together — the learned mix keeps a fifth of the ratchet's level. In real terms (the chain's gate reading, not the measure) nothing moves: +237 ± 470 and −61 ± 584. **Undercutting pays alone (§43's +275) and not in company (§45's half); a population learning in self-play walks into the company anyway** — each slot's own decision reads the undercut's award as its gain, and the price it gives up is spread over everyone, the slots included. The customers take the difference: tasks per 1,000 nominal credits +2.1 ± 0.3 and +2.2 ± 0.4 on 32.2 (7%; the all-undercut half +2.8 ± 0.6), the run's awards +144 ± 27 and +129 ± 30 on 2,449 (6%), the requesters' wallets 197 and 274 real against 109 — a little hoarded, the all-undercut's 440.

The loop: P_t at the close −1.1 ± 0.6 and −1.5 ± 0.6 (20.6 → 19.4, 19.1; the all-undercut half −2.8 ± 0.8 under the learner's construction, where §45's hand read −0.5 ± 0.8 reading the market once a tick — §46's recorded difference, the live read within the award pass), the members' price −1.4 ± 0.3 both; the floor does not bind (the state's price over the market 0.00 on every seed; the awards under the floor 2.9% against 4.0%, fewer — the undercut floored at cost takes awards the cheapest ratchets would have taken under 8); the state's share 0.2% and 0.6% against 0.9%; the false approvals +0.6 ± 1.1 and +1.6 ± 0.7 on 11.4 with 6% more awards reviewed — honesty unmoved; the arrivals unchanged (19.8), the departures +0.6 and +0.9 on 20.5 (the slots' own 4.4 and 4.7 against 4.3); the members idle for a bond 145 and 115 against 179.

**Read together.** The brief's §5 asked what a population of learners does to the price; the answer in run 24's world: the bidders in self-play take the price to 12 (three fifths of the ratchet's level) and hold it there, not to the cost of work (the all-undercut's 9, §45) and not back to the ratchet's 20 — one mix of the three actions, the same whether every member learns or half, so the equilibrium's level is set by how many members carry it, not by the policy adapting to the company. The members lose a third of their nominal surplus against holding the ratchet together (the half a sixteenth), the customers gain a quarter in tasks per credit and hoard the rest, the reservation sits beneath the market and does not bind, the state's share falls, honesty, arrivals and the floor's binding do not move. An evaluation under fixed rules; nothing asks for a rule. The order's next items — (ii) the reviewers in self-play, (iii) the bidders and requesters together — are not built; (iii) carries a construction to declare (the requesters' population has no exit and no standing, so the ruling's "one policy" is two, one per role, trained on their own decisions in one world).


## 48. The ruling after run 33, and the reviewers in self-play built (October 25, 2026; `540fab34`) — every member a learner slot; the basin test; honesty first

**The ruling (verbatim):** "Run 33 recorded; a reading, no rule; the price-taker limit of the attribution recorded (competitive adaptation tested; the collusive equilibrium the hand's ratchet, the reference). (ii) confirmed as declared, plus a basin test: self-play from the careful-weighted offline set and from a stamper-weighted one; references all-careful, all-stamper, run 24's 60/40; readings honesty first (false approvals, the plant record, suspensions, the audit layer's overturn rate), surplus and tasks per credit beside. (iii) two policies, one per role, accepted in principle; declare before launch."

**The price-taker limit, recorded.** §47's one mix whatever the company is the attribution's reading: a decision's reward is its own case's events, so a slot learns what its bid does to its own award and never what the population's bids do to the price — the per-decision attribution tests competitive adaptation (each slot a price-taker) and cannot find a collusive equilibrium; the hand's ratchet *is* that equilibrium, and stands as the reference. The construction's limit, as the ruling names it; not a fault to correct.

**(ii) The reviewers in self-play, built** (`--learner-pop all --learner-role reviewer --learner-exit rule`; the driver and the package only). The construction, one step past what I declared: **every member is a learner slot, the stampers included, and every arrival** — the ruling's three references (all-careful, all-stamper, run 24's 60/40) are three populations on the same slots, so the 60/40 is a reference and not the cell. Each slot is asked Q1's decision at each of its reviews (care at its cost / approve blind / reject blind) on its own observation (the 45 fields; Q2's seen-set fields now kept per slot), one policy answering every slot; the exit the population's rule, as in the bidders' cells (no boundary decision); the won case's events to its own decision, each slot's unclaimed events to its own lineage. The references are policies on the same slots: `careful` (every slot caring — all-careful), `stamper` (every slot approving blind — all-stamper), and `hand` — a fourth action code, 3, that the learner never has and the collectors never store: the slot answers as its disposition by hand would (a stamper stamps, a careful reviewer cares), so the hand reference is run 24's population on the identical slots. Checked: `hand` reproduces the control run to the decimal on seed 21 (P_t 20.1982, the members' price 20.2249, 8 false approvals, 2,409 awards, 21 departures, 13 suspensions, F̂ 0.0909); all-careful on the same seed reads P_t 13.3 with 4 false approvals and 3 suspensions (23 members alive against 19: the careful stay and compete), all-stamper P_t 30.2 with 14 standing false approvals, 51 suspensions, 9 alive and the state at 16% of the deliveries. 40 slots a run, ~4,400 decisions, 6 s an episode.

**The readings, honesty first** (run_q's population table for the reviewer role; paired against all-careful): the run's standing false approvals; the plant record — the bad plants approved and the good rejected over the run, the dial's F̂; the suspensions; **the audit layer's overturn rate** — of the settled roots that received an audit seat, the share whose initial approval the audit overturned (`audits_held`, `audits_overturning`, `audit_overturn_rate` in the row; the control reads 99 of 1,715, 5.8%, on seed 21); then the members' nominal surplus and the customers' tasks per 1,000 nominal credits (§46's measures), P_t and the members' price, the state's share, arrivals and departures, and the action mix by period (care / approve blind / reject blind). The env's plant and real stamp rates on the initial reviews and the blind-seat readings stay beside (meaningless for `hand`, whose action code is neither).

**The basin test, as ruled.** Two offline sets, two chains each: the **careful-weighted** set — careful, ε around careful, random on seeds 1–20 and the stamper on 1–5; the **stamper-weighted** set — the stamper, ε around the stamper (`eps-stamper`, new), random on 1–20 and the careful on 1–5. From each, ten self-play rounds of the direct (the carrier) and of the world model under the head alone (beside; stride 4 on per-slot windows — the CEM planner, Q1's instrument on one slot, is not run over 4,400 decisions an episode), the rounds' ε decisions drawn **uniformly in both** (Q1's rounds drew them from the careful policy; a basin test wants the exploration symmetric), the finals on seeds 21–40 against `careful`, `stamper`, `hand`. The question is whether the two starting points reach the same equilibrium, and whether it is honest. No calibration, as ruled. Launched at `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-careful/`, `learned-rev-stamper/`. The suite at `540fab34` in three modes. Read in §49.

**(iii), to declare before launch.** Two policies, one per role, in one world: the bidders' population (§46's construction, every careful member and careful arrival) and the requesters' population (every standing requester a slot of Q7's policy — the market rule's ceiling / patient / the floor — on its own observation, no exit and no standing), each trained in the rounds on its own role's decisions and evaluated together; the references the hand's pairs (ratchet × market rule — run 24's world; the §47 bidders' mix × market rule; ratchet × floor — §42's cell at every requester). The measures the population's: the members' nominal surplus, the customers' tasks per credit, P_t, the floor's binding. Not built; I will declare the construction in full after §49.


## 49. The reviewers in self-play, read (October 25, 2026; `results/redteam/stability/learned-rev-careful/`, `learned-rev-stamper/`) — one basin, and it is not honest: the population stamps half its reviews, ten times the hand's false approvals stand, and the audit layer confirms them

**The cell:** run 24's world (`--entry-pool 20 --stampers 0.6`, disputes off — the population's default), every member and every arrival a learner slot (39.9 slots a run) sharing one policy of Q1's three actions, the exit the population's rule; two offline sets (careful-weighted, stamper-weighted), from each ten self-play rounds of the direct (the carrier) and of the world model's head alone (beside), the rounds' ε uniform in both; the finals on seeds 21–40 against `careful` (all-careful), `stamper` (all-stamper) and `hand` (run 24's 60/40 on the same slots — the control to the decimal). Paired figures below are per seed, mean ± SE over 20.

**One basin.** From the careful-weighted set and from the stamper-weighted one, the direct and the head alone reach the same mix and hold it: **care 0.48, approve blind 0.51, reject blind 0.01** (the careful basin's direct 0.479 / 0.514 / 0.007 and head alone 0.497 / 0.499 / 0.004; the stamper basin's direct 0.482 / 0.508 / 0.010 and head alone 0.481 / 0.519 / 0.001). Round 5 reads the same as round 10 in every chain (0.478–0.517 care at round 5, 0.479–0.497 at round 10), and the rounds' training lifetime is steady from round 2 (the careful basin's direct 22,575 → 32,516 at round 2, then 30,737–33,410). Within a run the mix settles by the second period: the first period is 76–82% care (every slot pinned at genesis — see below), the second 34–39%, periods 2–13 hold between 0.38 and 0.46 care. The basin test is answered: the stamper-weighted start does not keep a stamping population and the careful-weighted start does not keep an honest one; both meet in the middle.

**Where the half is spent.** The population stamps the plant-blind seats almost entirely — 94–97% of the revised submissions and the panels (the audit seats among them), where no plant can come — and the initial reviews less than half the time: real cases 0.38–0.42, plants 0.26–0.31. It cares more where its standing is at stake and stamps more where the odds are lower: pinned (a probationer or an undetermined newcomer, odds 1 and re-checked at every plant), 60–63% care; a member at applied odds 0.7–1.0, 26–30% care; at odds below 0.7, 2–17% care (the band table: 0.83–0.98 stamps at odds 0.0–0.5). The odds at its stamps 0.865–0.879, at its cares 0.953–0.962, in all four chains. Q1's sleeper, which the single slot did not learn (§16), is the population's equilibrium: not a sleeper by the clock but by standing and odds — care while pinned, stamp as a member, stamp every seat a plant cannot reach.

**Honesty first.** Against the hand's 60/40 on the same slots (`hand`; its own figures beside), per run:

Nine to ten times the hand's standing false approvals, on every seed of 20 in every chain (the all-stamper's 16 is lower only because its members are suspended before they can pass more: 5.7 of 40 alive at the close, 746 awards, the state at 18%). The plant record catches what it can: the bad plants approved +76 to +86 a run, the suspensions +8.9 to +9.8 — and the population buys 6.0–6.9 standing false approvals per suspension where the all-stamper buys 2.4, because it stamps where the plants are not. **The audit layer does not catch the rest: with ten times the false approvals before it, its overturn rate is below the hand's** (−0.006 to −0.016, 2–5 SE) — the auditors are the same population, the audit seat is a plant-blind seat, and it is stamped 94–97% of the time. This is 1.2.0's capture (runs 2–3: the loop fair only with both challengers present and blind under capture) reached by learning in self-play: the plants hold the market alive where the all-stamper's dies, and the layer that was to catch the rest confirms it. Disputes are off in this cell (the population's default); §38's truthful disputes catch 89 of the 92 false approvals they contest in run 24's world — the challenger outside the reviewers' population, not tested here.

**Surplus and tasks per credit beside.**
